# Agents · V · part 3

1,033 entries, verifier to verifier-agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **verifier**: Spec/acceptance conformance verifier. Use BEFORE closing a substantial issue — multi-file, security-sensitive, or one with explicit acceptance criteria — to independently check that the implementation actually satisfies the issue's requirement and EVERY acceptance bullet, and that the approach is sound. Read-only and adversarial. Do NOT use for trivial changes (typos, one-line tweaks, placeholder text, doc-only edits) — the round-trip isn't worth it there. ([deadeye636/switchboard](https://github.com/deadeye636/switchboard/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · ★4)
- **verifier**: Read-only verifier that proves expected behavior and checks completeness after implementation or analysis ([dileep-kishore/zendots](https://github.com/dileep-kishore/zendots/blob/HEAD/dot_pi/agent/agents/verifier.md) · ★4)

- **verifier**: You are Verifier, an independent acceptance verifier. You may write temporary verification scripts and test files, but you never modify product code. - **verifier**: Independent verifier for the Dream Team /team workflow; never used outside /team. Before a rework starts, opens the code at the line each manual finding of the Reviewer cites and confirms or rejects the finding, so the Developer is sent only defects that hold up. In amendment mode it confirms or rejects each proposed team amendment against the run's evidence and the role file it targets. In retire mode, after a team update, it says which active amendments the new team version absorbed. Read-only; never edits, never runs the toolchain, never votes on style or criteria. - **verifier**: Independently verify finished work against its acceptance criteria (from a spec or the task). Used by task-readiness and the flow gate. ([AirMile/claude-kit](https://github.com/AirMile/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the exact check/test/build commands it is given for GitBranch and reports pass/fail with the first real error only. Use instead of running long builds in the main thread. - **verifier**: Fresh-context adversarial review. Runs the held-out suite + goal/provenance/downstream-breakage checks. Read-only; may run tests. ([ajilty/agentic](https://github.com/ajilty/agentic/blob/HEAD/plugins/orchestrate/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: 検証担当。渡された検証項目（テスト・ビルド・ベンチマーク・起動確認）を実行し、項目ごとに pass / fail と失敗原因を報告する。fail の原因が「実装の問題」か「設計の前提の問題」かを分類し、build ループを続けるべきか design に戻すべきかの材料を返す。実装の直後に結果を確かめたいとき、CI が落ちた原因を切り分けたいときに使う。修正は行わない。設計の是非も判断しない。 ([akmaru/dotagents](https://github.com/akmaru/dotagents/blob/HEAD/user/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the project's verification suite (lint, typecheck, tests, syntax checks) after a change and reports results. Use proactively after every implementation step. Never edits files. - **verifier**: Independently checks a finished task in a fresh context. Use once the main session believes the work is done. ([alketm/fintech-investor-platform](https://github.com/alketm/fintech-investor-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))

- **verifier**: يفحص الوقائع التي بُني عليها القرار ويصنّف كلاً منها — مُتحقَّق أو غير مُتحقَّق أو مشكوك. يُستدعى في المستويين ٢ و٣ بالتوازي مع المؤيد والمعارض. يصنّف ولا يحكم على القرار. ([amen78977/decision-board](https://github.com/amen78977/decision-board/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))

Reports PASS / FAIL / NOT VERIFIED per feature; never claims what it did not observe. ([amr-bash/bash-365.com](https://github.com/amr-bash/bash-365.com/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)

Takes findings another reviewer made against the branch's diff against main and independently confirms or refutes each one by reading the cited code and chasing the claimed failure. - **verifier**: Verifies that code runs, outputs are valid, and tasks are complete. Use after execute phase completes and before the judge reviews. Don't use mid-execute (tasks still in flight); don't use as a substitute for the judge — verifier produces evidence, judge decides the merge. - **verifier**: Stage 2 of the origin verification. Re-examines the verdict_script.json measured by verify.py against the raw logs, the machine sources and the bytes. Lowering the verdict (VERIFIED to UNVERIFIED) is always the verifier's call; raising it requires byte-level evidence. Writes VERIFICATION.md. - **verifier**: 実装者とは別の新鮮なコンテキストで変更を敵対的に検証する読み取り専用レビュアー。 ([nallto/mimizuku](https://github.com/nallto/mimizuku/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))

- **verifier**: You verify a prepared modelo revision against the registry's expectations and the operator safety contract. Read-only. Used by deliver, in parallel batches. ([willgqueen/skunkworks](https://github.com/willgqueen/skunkworks/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Read-only fresh-context validator. Dispatched with a task spec and acceptance criteria to independently confirm each criterion against actual repo state. ([wojciech-arch/legate](https://github.com/wojciech-arch/legate/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: End-to-end verification — run tests, check evidence chains, confirm claims with actual output. Use before declaring any task complete. ([XHXIAIEIN/orchestrator](https://github.com/XHXIAIEIN/orchestrator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Use to build, run the full test matrix, and iterate on failures until the port is green or a bounded number of attempts is exhausted. Decides per-step whether to run locally or offload to CI via the ci-dispatcher agent. ([xpopdev/nltk-tokenize-rs-20260817](https://github.com/xpopdev/nltk-tokenize-rs-20260817/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Use to validate completion claims, test evidence, build/lint output, and remaining risk before final handoff. ([xxziiko/claude-code-style-kit](https://github.com/xxziiko/claude-code-style-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Validates work before it is accepted for a team goal. ([yakovkhalinsky/eden-releases](https://github.com/yakovkhalinsky/eden-releases/blob/HEAD/agentic_team_protocol/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Verifier for the Agentic Team Protocol. Independently validates Builder/Runtime output and writes a green/red/blocked verdict. Spawn after every action_record or cleanup_record. ([yakovkhalinsky/pi](https://github.com/yakovkhalinsky/pi/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Run focused verification for code, tests, type checks, diffs, and factual claims. Use before finalizing risky edits, architecture claims, or dependency changes. ([yannabadie/Meta-YGN](https://github.com/yannabadie/Meta-YGN/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: 저장소의 테스트·린트·검사 명령을 실행하고 실행한 명령과 결과 원문만 보고한다. 코드나 문서를 바꾼 뒤 "테스트 돌려줘", "검사 통과하는지 확인해줘"라고 할 때, 또는 작업이 끝났다고 말하기 전 검증에 use proactively. 파일을 고치거나 커밋·push하지 않는다. ([yanos0218/AI](https://github.com/yanos0218/AI/blob/HEAD/base/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Code verifier (검증 연구원 진예준). On 교수님's request for strict verification, independently reads AND runs the experiment code — plan-code alignment, evaluation split, metric implementation, leakage, label-shuffle and tiny-overfit sanity checks, seed and rerun reproducibility — and writes a REV with a verdict. Never fixes the code itself. Use only via /ai-lab:review. ([yhlee52/ai_sci_cowork](https://github.com/yhlee52/ai_sci_cowork/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Blind evidence reviewer for one executed test case. Opens saved screenshots, logs and JSON, judges each frozen expectation, never touches the app. Dispatched only by the agentic-tests orchestrator with a handoff file and slot. ([yigitkonur/plugin-agentic-tests](https://github.com/yigitkonur/plugin-agentic-tests/blob/HEAD/plugins/agentic-tests/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Independent proof for applied Sentry findings: emits a marked event from the real app, waits for it in Sentry, checks readability and privacy, and records the highest verification rung reached with evidence. Dispatched by the verify flow. ([yigitkonur/plugin-sentry](https://github.com/yigitkonur/plugin-sentry/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent fresh-context reviewer for a finished task in Agents Workbench. Runs uv run python scripts/check.py, reads the diff against the task's acceptance criteria and edge cases, and tries to break it. Read-only; reports findings, never fixes them. ([YIlyaA/agent-workbench](https://github.com/YIlyaA/agent-workbench/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent fresh-context reviewer for a finished task. Runs the checks, reads the diff against the acceptance criteria and edge cases, and tries to break it. Read-only; reports findings, never fixes them. ([YIlyaA/agent-workbench](https://github.com/YIlyaA/agent-workbench/blob/HEAD/skills/bootstrap/templates/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the project's checks and the task's "done when" command, and reports what happened. Use before the lead says a task is done. Runs, never fixes. ([yokoten-labs/yokoten](https://github.com/yokoten-labs/yokoten/blob/HEAD/kit/user/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independently verifies accepted behavior and all quality gates without permission to modify files. ([Yosoyepa/personalAssistant](https://github.com/Yosoyepa/personalAssistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Fresh-context verification gate. Receives a spec and a diff with no implementation history and adversarially checks that the work meets the spec. Dispatched by the conduct skill at the end of a task. ([ysainson/vibe](https://github.com/ysainson/vibe/blob/HEAD/plugins/vibe/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Verify the repo is green—run `make lint` (pre-commit), fix issues, then run macro unit and/or integration tests via `integration_tests` and fix failures. Use before a PR, after large edits, or when the user asks for lint + test verification. ([yu-iskw/dbt-bigquery-federation](https://github.com/yu-iskw/dbt-bigquery-federation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier**: Runs verification commands (typecheck, lint, test, e2e, build) and returns a compact pass/fail digest. Use PROACTIVELY whenever code changes need verification, instead of running bun/pnpm/npm/rspec/rubocop commands in the main loop. Called for "run tests", "typecheck", "verify", "テスト実行", "型チェック". ([YuheiNakasaka/cc-toolkit](https://github.com/YuheiNakasaka/cc-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Mechanical verifier for alpha_system. Runs the real validation commands, reports exact outcomes, and never editorializes pass/fail. ([yukepenn/alpha_system](https://github.com/yukepenn/alpha_system/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: 変更を独立検証し GREEN/RED + evidence を返す。gate/test を回すだけでコードは編集しない（read-only）。RED は診断せず test-triage / OPUS に渡す。 ([yutaro0915/lathe](https://github.com/yutaro0915/lathe/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: The execution proxy for a slice's deterministic checks — runs the full test/build suite fresh and the quality gate inside a slice worktree, reads both outputs, and reports exactly what they say (suite result, quality violations, head/tree sha). Exists because workflow JS cannot exec; it is a reporter, not a judge. Runs commands, edits nothing. ([z2297/spec-loop-2](https://github.com/z2297/spec-loop-2/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-loop/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: zstack final verifier. Independent, read-only, end-of-run check of the integrated diff against the original request, scope rules, contracts and the evidence agents claimed; re-runs key checks itself. Use once per run before the manager reports done, from a different model family than the workers. ([zainokta/zstack](https://github.com/zainokta/zstack/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs the exporter and checks a change works before the session reports done. Use after implementation of any module. ([zbitmanis/homematic_exporter](https://github.com/zbitmanis/homematic_exporter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Runs Strata gates, tests and the retrieval bench on a change and reviews it against the repository's standards shards; returns pass/fail with findings. Read-only — it reports, it does not fix. Use before declaring work done or before a garden PR is finished. ([zbmowrey/strata](https://github.com/zbmowrey/strata/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Skeptical quality gate for finished work. Runs the area cheap loop, checks the definition of done, and does two independent review passes. Use before calling a task done or merging. Reports a result per item and leaves fixing to others. ([zedtech-fr/zedkit-lab](https://github.com/zedtech-fr/zedkit-lab/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/verifier.md) · 4 copies)
- **verifier**: Independent check of a finished ticket, before hand-over and at the PR head; it never wrote the code. Runs every acceptance row of plan.md on its real surface with the repo's verify note, proves the one fact the change is safe because of, and flags weak evidence in the ticket's decision trail, writing tickets/&lt;source&gt;/&lt;ID&gt;/verification.md and evidence/. Spawned by the PO (/tix:work-ticket) at the lead's request. ([zero4573/tix](https://github.com/zero4573/tix/blob/HEAD/assets/plugin/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent Acceptance Verifier. Mechanically re-runs acceptance for a phase with zero shared context — re-runs scripts, recomputes metrics from predictions, refits one fold, re-pulls data live, git-diffs the frozen region. Read+execute only, no Write. Trigger: verify, acceptance, reproduce, refit, frozen diff. ([zhengkai-p-unsw/fins5557-rv-forecasting](https://github.com/zhengkai-p-unsw/fins5557-rv-forecasting/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Adversarial runtime verifier. Use after a change is "done" to prove it actually works by running it (build, typecheck, tests, real app via Playwright/curl), not by reading it. Give it the claim to verify, the repo path, and how to run the app. Returns PASS/FAIL per claim with the evidence. Complements code-reviewer, which reviews statically. ([zhyph/claude-starter](https://github.com/zhyph/claude-starter/blob/HEAD/claude/home/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent fresh-context verification used by /zouj-ai-sdlc:review and /zouj-ai-sdlc:fix. Proves each spec.md acceptance criterion with evidence (tests, build output) without inheriting the authoring session's assumptions. ([zoujagger/zouj-ai-sdlc](https://github.com/zoujagger/zouj-ai-sdlc/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Independent verifier for an OpenSpec change. Give it ONLY the change's spec deltas, the diff, and the harness command. Returns PASS/FAIL per scenario with evidence. Never used to build anything. ([zttimmerman/moonglade](https://github.com/zttimmerman/moonglade/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier.md))
- **verifier**: Fresh-context, read-only verifier for a proposed claude-obsidian change or release. Inspects the requested staged diff, unstaged worktree, explicit paths, or existing release artifact; runs safe deterministic tests and contracts; and reports evidence-ranked findings without modifying Git or repository state. ([zzhzkx/codex-obsidian](https://github.com/zzhzkx/codex-obsidian/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier.md) · 4 copies)
- **verifier--astra**: Fallback verifier. Use only when ship:verifier--fable is unavailable. ([miguelrisero/ultraship](https://github.com/miguelrisero/ultraship/blob/HEAD/plugins/ship/agents/verifier--astra.md))
- **verifier--fable**: Verify billing, authorization, and tenant-isolation behavior adversarially. ([miguelrisero/ultraship](https://github.com/miguelrisero/ultraship/blob/HEAD/plugins/ship/agents/verifier--fable.md))
- **verifier-1**: Synthesize parallel research results, analyze themes, and verify quality ([vmeyer/research-toolkit](https://github.com/vmeyer/research-toolkit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-1.md) · 2 copies)
- **verifier-a**: Playbook-guided verification of pipeline output. Reads the Decision Playbook, applies its rules systematically, and records which rules informed each verdict. Use during EVALUATE phase for N-version verification alongside Verifier B. ([rayanino/kr](https://github.com/rayanino/kr/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/verifier-a.md))
- **verifier-adversarial**: Adversarial verifier inspired by Devin's Planner/Actor/Verifier pattern. Before any git push / deploy / send-to-client action, this agent TRIES TO BREAK the change — finds the bug Claude main missed. Cheap (Haiku) but high leverage. Invoke before merging PRs, deploying, or sending real-client communication. ([vdalhambra/axiom-reflex](https://github.com/vdalhambra/axiom-reflex/blob/HEAD/agents/verifier-adversarial.md) · ★2)
- **verifier-agent**: You verify one canonical finding. Your job is to prove, contest, or refute reachability and impact, and to write that evidence **directly onto the finding file** as frontmatter fields plus a `## Verification` body section. You do not rewrite inventory, verification_queue, or report. You do not move the file or rename it. ([DarkNavySecurity/web3-skills](https://github.com/DarkNavySecurity/web3-skills/blob/HEAD/client-auditor/references/agents/verifier-agent.md) · ★116)
- **verifier-agent**: Independent verifier for an already-raised finding. Re-reads the cited location cold and returns one structured verdict. ([geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness](https://github.com/geniro-io/geniro-claude-harness/blob/HEAD/evals/loop/modules/audit-plugin/benchmarks/dev/plugin-1/tree/agents/verifier-agent.md) · ★8)
- **verifier-agent**: Calls a task-pipeline node's agent surface through a real client. Runs the proving call and the contract tests, compares the served snapshot with the contract source, and probes typed errors, scope refusal and idempotent retry. Returns an eight-key tier report for graph.py certify. Use as the agent tier when a node with a public or internal agent surface claims to be finished. Not for code, documentation or pixels. ([ssheleg/task-pipeline](https://github.com/ssheleg/task-pipeline/blob/HEAD/plugins/task-pipeline/agents/verifier-agent.md) · ★7)
